# Three nonlinear correlated laws: complete analytical screens

Status: JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT, UNVERIFIED. Worker max2, invocation60,
under TASK.md, plan e570a4da874393de, master_guidance ca0fd834c90cd90a
and elaboration f5f79a115a09ff55. The original exact-profile problem is OPEN.
No mathematical program, numerical evaluation, arithmetic replay,
optimizer, solver, executed trajectory or recurrence, benchmark DP,
formal submission, installation, consult or subagent was used.

This is one allocated analytical round, after ended baseline59, with
ended ceiling60/attempted ceiling61. It does not renew itself.

## 1. What was screened, and what the conclusions mean

Three explicit laws leave the old aligned affine elapsed-clock class.

1. A parabolic longitudinal mean has a nonzero initial transverse
   gradient and a transverse sign reversal. A path of ORIGINAL rows
   from the initial optimum constraint through every physical step
   forces every legal radius1 payoff below 1/[1+(8/3)S] whenever
   max h_t <= S/2. This includes the unchanged tau=1/4 hierarchy.
   The terminal row alone is not an adequate screening test here.
2. Accumulated centered projective-incidence innovations have an exact
   real Gram, a parabolic longitudinal mean and an arbitrary initial
   transverse offset. The palindrome moment identity pays the complete
   terminal covariance charge. If the offset makes that charge negative,
   the first original two-row cycle forces a larger scalar payment.
   Under explicit conditions containing the assigned hierarchy, every
   legal radius1 payoff is strictly below the same-word Huber payoff.
3. One static nonorthogonal Huber ridge plus a quadratic background is
   globally legal, sharp1-smooth and has a unique minimum. It has actual
   queried gate changes, noncommuting Hessians and a terminal gradient
   with negative initial-radius component. A fully explicit three-step
   bridge beats the same-word Huber value. A different four-step word
   sends the entire fixed scale family to its minimum exactly. Zero
   padding therefore defeats that whole family at arbitrarily large n.

The first two are rejected by NECESSARY original-row inequalities before
asserting that their entire proposed data arrays are realizable. No
expected-feasibility or unsigned-Gram argument is used to claim a
function. The third is an actual static function, with every original
row, the terminal value, and the true closest radius proved below.
The first two upper bounds apply only to their declared origin-anchored,
radius1 law classes. They are not upper bounds on unrestricted W_n(h).
The third annihilator is a counterexample to that fixed witness family,
not a small-error upper bound on unrestricted W_n for its word.

Every new assertion is proved jointly here and remains unverified.
No unverified shared finding is a mathematical premise. Accepted
1252b8823bb5a290 supplies interpolation necessity/sufficiency and its
explicit conjugate construction. Accepted5fd396eba75651e7 is used ONLY
for its Section10 original benchmark comparison. Acceptedb98bb807d514e80d
is used for the original benchmark definitions and scalar single-cut
comparison. Accepted0e7e78056dc6258c was read to avoid returning to its
expected-orthogonality class; its upper bound is not applied to these laws.

## 2. The original word, all directed rows, and an explicit data convention

Normalize L=1 and take the optimum sample x_star=g_star=f_star=0.
For arbitrary finite h=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)) >=0 define

    S=sum_(t<n)h_t, P_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, q_i=P_i/S.        (1)

For S>0, q_0=0, q_n=1, and q_(i+1)-q_i=h_i/S. Zero steps
repeat q and must repeat positions, gradients and values. All expressions
below are written finite sums; none was executed by a mathematical program.
For S=0 one can use the separate static fallback F(x)=||x||^2/2,
x_0=e0, with every iterate unchanged, unique minimum0 and payoff1.
That degenerate fallback repairs no failure on the positive-mass words.

For any displayed normalized gradient-vector law v_i and scale sigma>0,
use the complete original physical arrays

    g_i=sigma*v_i,
    D_i^nc=sum_(t<i)h_t*v_t,
    x_i=e0-sigma*D_i^nc,                 0<=i<=n.     (2)

These satisfy x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i*g_i, including every zero step.
The original signed interpolation residual, with its original orientation,
is

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.       (3)

EVERY ordered distinct pair in {star,0,...,n} is retained. In particular,
no incoming terminal or remote queried-prefix row is discarded.

Here is an explicit common value convention for attempted laws1 and2:

    C_i^val=sum_(t=i)^(n-1) (h_t/2)*<v_t+v_(t+1),v_t>,
    f_i=tau_val+sigma^2*C_i^val, C_n^val=0.           (4)

For these values every finite ordered row is EXACTLY

    Q_ij/sigma^2 = C_i^val-C_j^val
                  +<v_i,D_i^nc-D_j^nc>+||v_i-v_j||^2/2. (5)

Both optimum directions are EXACTLY

    Q_(i,star)=tau_val-sigma*<v_i,e0>
        +sigma^2*(C_i^val+<v_i,D_i^nc>+||v_i||^2/2),
    Q_(star,i)=-tau_val+sigma^2*(||v_i||^2/2-C_i^val). (6)

Thus the full optimum-value interval, not only the terminal upper row, is

    lower_i=sigma^2*(||v_i||^2/2-C_i^val),
    upper_i=sigma*<v_i,e0>
         -sigma^2*(C_i^val+<v_i,D_i^nc>+||v_i||^2/2),
    max_i lower_i <= tau_val <= min_i upper_i.        (7)

If all finite rows(5) pass, an explicit maximal choice is
tau_val=min_i upper_i, provided it is at least max_i lower_i.
These formulas specify every position, gradient, value and original-row
cost, rather than supplying separately chosen values for different tests.
For any directed finite cycle, its exact cost is the sum of the displayed
terms in(5); the C_i^val terms telescope. For cycles involving star,
use(5),(6), where tau_val also telescopes. No unknown expectation enters.

Only if ALL those signs pass may the accepted interpolation construction
be invoked. Explicitly, with the zero jet included, put

    K(v)=max{||v||^2/2,
             max_(0<=i<=n)[<x_i,v>-f_i+||v-g_i||^2/2]},
    F(x)=sup_v[<x,v>-K(v)].                           (8)

Fact1252b8823bb5a290 proves that passing all rows realizes these exact
jets by ONE everywhere finite convex differentiable1-smooth F with
minimum0. The Gram is the Gram of the actual displayed Euclidean vectors.
If additionally tau_val>max_i lower_i, every nonzero-jet quadratic is
strictly below the zero-jet quadratic at v=0. Finitely many continuous
quadratics then leave K(v)=||v||^2/2 in a neighborhood of0. The
subdifferential of K at0 is {0}, so argmin F={0}. Consequently the true
closest initial distance is ||e0||=1, and smoothness1 is sharp locally.

Laws1 and2 require that strict condition for a claimed TRUE-radius1
realization. No existence of a passing array is assumed in their
performance screens. Their bounds actually allow ALL common values,
not merely(4). Thus changing(4) cannot repair the stated performance
failures. If a different realization has a closer minimum, the present
origin-radius upper bound is not asserted for that different normalization.

For advertised L>0 use L*F and physical steps h_i/L. The normalized
payoff is still 2f_n/dist(x0,argmin F)^2, exactly W=2R.

## 3. Law1: a parabolic mean and a transverse sign reversal

In R^2, with orthonormal e0,e1, choose the fixed coefficients

    delta_par=1/4, nu_par=27/8, b_par=9/10,
    v_i^par=(1-delta_par*q_i^2)e0
                         +sqrt(nu_par)*(q_i-b_par)e1. (9)

Use(2) for all positions. Written componentwise these are

    x_i=[1-sigma*P_i+sigma*delta_par*U_i]e0
                       -sigma*sqrt(nu_par)*(J_i-b_par*P_i)e1,
    U_i=sum_(t<i)h_t*q_t^2, J_i=sum_(t<i)h_t*q_t.     (10)

Use(4)--(7) for one explicit value array and its ALL-row/cycle costs.
The full gradient Gram and the initial-gradient correlations are

    <e0,g_i>=sigma*(1-q_i^2/4),
    <g_i,g_j>=sigma^2*[(1-q_i^2/4)(1-q_j^2/4)
                         +(27/8)(q_i-9/10)(q_j-9/10)]. (11)

This is a real PSD rank2 Gram, not independent directions. At i=0 the
transverse component is negative and nonzero. At i=n it is positive.
The longitudinal mean is quadratic in elapsed mass, so this is not
another scalar retuning of the affine-clock law. Initial gradient and
initial radius are not aligned.

### 3.1 The original optimum-to-terminal path pays the gain

Consider ANY common values which make these gradients and positions legal
with the origin optimum and x0=e0. The row Q_(0,star)<=0 gives

    f_0<=sigma-sigma^2*||v_0^par||^2/2.

For every physical t, the actual original row Q_(t+1,t)<=0 gives

    f_(t+1)<=f_t-h_t*sigma^2*<v_(t+1)^par,v_t^par>
                              -sigma^2*||v_(t+1)^par-v_t^par||^2/2.

Summing ALL these rows, including the terminal row at t=n-1, yields

    2f_n<=2sigma-D_path*sigma^2,
    D_path=||v_0^par||^2
       +2sum_t h_t*<v_(t+1)^par,v_t^par>
       +sum_t||v_(t+1)^par-v_t^par||^2.              (12)

This is the concrete replacement for the terminal-only screen. It uses
the initial optimum row, every directed physical predecessor row and
the full finite-amplitude vector law. No value re-selection avoids it.

### 3.2 Exact cross quadrature, without a trajectory evaluation

For a written partition interval [q,q+d] with 0<=q<=q+d<=1, define

    v(q)=(1-delta_par*q^2)e0+sqrt(nu_par)*(q-b_par)e1.

Polynomial integration gives the EXACT identity

    integral_q^(q+d)||v(t)||^2 dt -d*<v(q),v(q+d)>
       =d^3*[(delta_par+nu_par)/3
                      +delta_par^2*(q^2+q*d+d^2/5)]. (13)

For the transverse term, this is the equality
integral(q-b_par)^2-d(q-b_par)(q+d-b_par)=d^3/3.
For the longitudinal term, expanding 1-2delta_par*t^2+delta_par^2*t^4
gives delta_par*d^3/3+delta_par^2*d^3*(q^2+qd+d^2/5).
Thus(13) retains the entire error, not a Taylor remainder.

Since q+d<=1, q^2+qd+d^2/5<=1. Also

    integral_0^1||v(t)||^2dt
       =1-2delta_par/3+delta_par^2/5
                        +nu_par*(1/3-b_par+b_par^2)
       =4001/2400>5/3,
    (delta_par+nu_par)/3+delta_par^2=61/48<4/3.       (14)

Write d_t=h_t/S and d_max=max_t d_t. Because sum_t d_t=1,
sum_t d_t^3<=d_max^2. Summing(13) over the EXACT original partition,
with zero intervals contributing zero, proves

    (1/S)sum_t h_t*<v_t^par,v_(t+1)^par>
                    >5/3-(4/3)*d_max^2.             (15)

If max h_t<=S/2, the right side is at least4/3. Furthermore
||v_0^par||^2=1+nu_par*b_par^2>1 and the last sum in(12) is nonnegative.
Consequently

    D_path>1+(8/3)S,
    2f_n<=1/D_path<1/[1+(8/3)S]<1/(1+2S)=H_base(h). (16)

The middle inequality follows by completing the square in(12):
2sigma-D_path*sigma^2=1/D_path-D_path*(sigma-1/D_path)^2.
The bound is over ALL scales and ALL legal common values. In particular
it also holds for every successful strict-radius1 realization in(8).

### 3.3 Exact original benchmark comparison, at the actual horizon

Keep the assigned enclosing parameters, without substituting those of a child:

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta_word=1-1/(4m),
    c=lambda*(1+1/(4m)), a_word=beta_word/c,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a_word*c^j,H_j),
    h=H_m, n=2^m-1, m>=1536.                         (17)

The word is a palindrome and its largest entry is the central
1+a_word*c^(m-1). It satisfies max h_t<S/2. To check the latter
without any mass computation, c<3 and m>=3 give

    2S_(m-1)>=2*(1+a_word*c^(m-2))
                         +4*(1+a_word*c^(m-3)),
    2S_(m-1)-(1+a_word*c^(m-1))
       >=5+a_word*c^(m-3)*(2c+4-c^2)>0.             (18)

Here 2c+4-c^2>0 on 1<c<3. Since S=2S_(m-1)+h_central,
the desired largest-step comparison follows.

The ORIGINAL benchmark is U_n^ZJ=1/B_n with ALL split indices retained.
Accepted5fd396eba75651e7, Section10, gives at THIS actual n

    B_n*H_base(H_m)<237/250,                         (19)

so every legal law1 radius1 payoff V satisfies the exact comparison

    V/U_n^ZJ=B_n*V
        < [B_n*H_base(H_m)]*(1+2S)/[1+(8/3)S]
        <237/250<1.                                 (20)

Law1 therefore cannot match the benchmark even on this original full
word. This rejects its fixed nonlinear coefficients; it excludes no
arbitrary nonlinear mean law or terminal longitudinal reversal.

## 4. Law2: accumulated real finite-field covariance with initial memory

### 4.1 The incidence design is a real simplex, with its metric proved

Choose a prime p_ff such that N_ff=p_ff^2+p_ff+1>=n+1, and put
d_ff=N_ff-1. Such a prime exists: any prime divisor of (n+1)!+1 is
greater than n+1. This is an existence argument, not a performed
factorial evaluation or prime search.

Over the field Z/p_ff Z, projective points are nonzero triples modulo
nonzero scalar multiples. Each class has p_ff-1 representatives, giving
N_ff=(p_ff^3-1)/(p_ff-1) points. A projective line is a nonzero linear
form modulo nonzero scalar multiples, with incidence defined by the
vanishing of that form. There are N_ff lines as well.

The kernel of one nonzero linear form has p_ff^2 elements. Removing zero
and passing to scalar classes gives p_ff+1 points per line. Two distinct
line forms are linearly independent; their common kernel has dimension1,
so their lines share exactly one projective point. These counts follow
by eliminating a coordinate with a nonzero coefficient, and eliminating
two coordinates for the pair. No external finite-geometry theorem is
being imported.

In REAL R^(N_ff), let 1_L be the incidence vector of line L and 1 the
all-ones vector. Put k_ff=p_ff+1 and

    u_L^ff=[1_L-(k_ff/N_ff)*1]
                     /sqrt(k_ff-k_ff^2/N_ff).        (21)

Their real inner products, by the proved counts, are exactly

    ||u_L^ff||^2=1,
    <u_L^ff,u_M^ff>=-1/d_ff for L!=M.               (22)

Indeed the distinct numerator inner product is
1-k_ff^2/N_ff=-p_ff/N_ff, while the squared denominator is
(p_ff+1)*p_ff^2/N_ff. Their quotient is -1/[p_ff(p_ff+1)].
Their Gram is therefore PSD because these are explicitly real vectors;
there is no conversion of a finite-field bilinear form into an assumed
Euclidean metric. For arbitrary real coefficients z_L the exact norm is

    ||sum_L z_L*u_L^ff||^2
      =[N_ff*sum_L z_L^2-(sum_L z_L)^2]/d_ff.        (23)

It is nonnegative also directly by Cauchy--Schwarz, extending a selected
subset's coefficients by zero to the N_ff lines.

Choose n distinct step lines and one further line, denoted u_t^ff
(0<=t<n) and u_extra^ff. Add a unit e0 orthogonal to R^(N_ff).

### 4.2 An explicit nonlinear all-history law

Choose sigma>0, nu_inc>0, 0<delta_inc<=1/2 and an ARBITRARY real
initial offset b_inc. Define

    z_i^inc=b_inc*u_extra^ff+(1/S)sum_(t<i)h_t*u_t^ff,
    v_i^inc=(1-delta_inc*q_i^2)e0+sqrt(nu_inc)*z_i^inc. (24)

Use ALL physical arrays(2) and the full common-value convention(4)--(7).
In coordinates the positions are

    x_i=[1-sigma*P_i+sigma*delta_inc*U_i]e0
                        -sigma*sqrt(nu_inc)*sum_(t<i)h_t*z_t^inc,
    U_i=sum_(t<i)h_t*q_t^2.                          (25)

Zero steps add no innovation, so repeated queries have identical gradients.
The covariance is time dependent and retains every earlier innovation.
It is not an independent fresh-gradient construction or an AR nearest-
neighbor assumption. The initial gradient has a transverse component
unless b_inc=0. Opposite temporal inner products are allowed.

Write E_i=sum_(t<i)h_t^2, and r_min=min(i,j). The exact covariance is

    <z_i^inc,z_j^inc>=b_inc^2
      -b_inc*(q_i+q_j)/d_ff
      +[N_ff*E_(r_min)/S^2-q_i*q_j]/d_ff.            (26)

The entire Gram of (x0,g0,...,gn), including all initial correlations,
is determined by(24)--(26), with

    <x0,g_i>=sigma*(1-delta_inc*q_i^2),
    <g_i,g_j>=sigma^2*[(1-delta_inc*q_i^2)
                       *(1-delta_inc*q_j^2)
                              +nu_inc*<z_i^inc,z_j^inc>]. (27)

The initial displacement is EXACTLY e0; a successful strict realization
of(8) has unique minimum0 and true radius1. No minimum is moved without
changing these law hypotheses. All pair/cycle costs are(5),(6) with
(24), not an expected sign or an unspecified combinatorial feasibility
claim.

### 4.3 Complete terminal charge, with every covariance term retained

Let A_inc=1-delta_inc>0 and set

    U=sum_t h_t*q_t^2<=S/3,
    T=sum_t h_t*P_t=(S^2-E)/2, E=sum_t h_t^2,
    eta=T/S, K_inc=1+S+eta, M=1+2S.

The bound on U is the left-endpoint inequality on each interval of the
nondecreasing function(P/S)^2, whose integral from0 to S is S/3.
The formula for T is obtained by expanding the square of sum h.

The original row Q_(n,star)<=0, for ANY legal common values, yields

    2f_n<=2sigma*A_inc-D_inc*sigma^2,
    D_inc=M*A_inc^2+2delta_inc*A_inc*(S-U)
                                   +nu_inc*C_inc(b_inc),
    C_inc(b)=||z_n^inc||^2+2sum_t h_t*<z_n^inc,z_t^inc>.
                                                            (28)

Direct use of(26) gives the complete initial-offset polynomial

    C_inc(b)=M*b^2-2K_inc*b/d_ff+C_inc(0),
    C_inc(0)=[N_ff*(E+2J_inc)/S^2-1-2eta]/d_ff,
    J_inc=sum_t h_t^2*sum_(r>t)h_r.                 (29)

No positive and negative cross term has been discarded. In particular
allowing initial transverse correlation can make C_inc(b) negative;
asserting nonnegativity for every b would be a wrong shortcut.

### 4.4 A palindrome identity pays the zero-offset part

For a palindrome h, reflection of the exact physical indices gives

    2J_inc=S*E-F3, F3=sum_t h_t^3.                 (30)

Proof: sum h_t^2 times prefix mass equals the suffix-mass sum by
reflection. Adding those sums gives sum h_t^2(S-h_t)=S E-F3.

Put p_t=h_t/S, e=sum p_t^2, f=sum p_t^3. For ANY nonnegative
p_t with sum p_t=1 the following exact identity holds:

    (n+1)e-nf-1
       =sum_(i<j)(p_i-p_j)^2*(1-p_i-p_j)>=0.       (31)

To verify it, sum(p_i-p_j)^2=n e-1 and
sum(p_i-p_j)^2(p_i+p_j)=n f-e. Subtract these two equalities.
Each last factor is nonnegative because all remaining p's are nonnegative.
Since N_ff>=n+1, e-f>=0 and N_ff e>=1, it follows that

    (N_ff+1)e-N_ff f-1>=0, N_ff E-S^2>=0.

Multiplying the numerator of C_inc(0) in(29) by S^2 and using(30)
gives exactly

    N_ff*E-S^2
      +S*[(N_ff+1)E-N_ff*F3/S-S^2]>=0.             (32)

Thus C_inc(0)>=0 for EVERY palindrome, including zero entries. This is
a proved coefficient inequality, not covariance domination by an
unverified temporal kernel.

### 4.5 The first original two-row cycle pays a negative initial-offset charge

Let h_lead be the first positive physical step. All earlier zero steps
leave q=0, z=b_inc*u_extra^ff and x=e0. Summing the TWO original rows
at this actual step cancels their values and gives exactly

    Q_(lead,lead+1)+Q_(lead+1,lead)
      =sigma^2*(h_lead/S)^2
          [delta_inc^2*(h_lead/S)^2
                    +nu_inc*(1-b_inc*S/d_ff)-delta_inc*h_lead]. (33)

The innovation has length h_lead/S, and its inner product with the
initial transverse offset is -b_inc/d_ff. This proves(33) directly,
including the initial correlation term instead of presuming it vanishes.
Legal original rows require the square bracket to be nonpositive.

Assume the explicit screen conditions

    h is a positive-mass palindrome, S>=2,
    0<h_lead<=2, d_ff>=4, S<=d_ff^2/4.              (34)

If C_inc(b_inc)>=0, (28) immediately gives D_inc>M*A_inc^2.
If C_inc(b_inc)<0, (29),(32) imply

    0<b_inc<2K_inc/(d_ff*M)<=2/d_ff,

because eta<=S/2 makes K_inc<=M. Consequently
1-b_inc*S/d_ff>=1-2S/d_ff^2>=1/2. The necessary original cycle(33)
then forces

    delta_inc*h_lead>=nu_inc/2,
    delta_inc>=nu_inc/4.                            (35)

Meanwhile completing the square in(29) gives

    C_inc(b_inc)>=-K_inc^2/(d_ff^2*M)>=-M/d_ff^2.

Since A_inc>=1/2, S-U>=2S/3 and S>=2, the COMPLETE correction in(28)
is bounded below, using(35), by

    2delta_inc*A_inc*(S-U)+nu_inc*C_inc(b_inc)
      >=nu_inc*S/6-nu_inc*M/d_ff^2
      >=nu_inc*S*(1/6-5/32)=nu_inc*S/96>0.          (36)

Here M=1+2S<=(5/2)S and d_ff>=4. Thus BOTH covariance phases give

    D_inc>M*A_inc^2,
    2f_n<=A_inc^2/D_inc<1/M=H_base(h).              (37)

Completion of the square in(28) proves the second line for every sigma.
This is a uniform screen over b_inc in ALL of R, every nu_inc>0 and
0<delta_inc<=1/2, and all legal common values. The coefficient payment
is not an amplitude or value tuning inside the old affine clock.

### 4.6 Conditions and exact benchmark on the original full hierarchy

For h=H_m in(17), h_lead=1+a_word<2 and the palindrome property holds.
We have a_word<=c-2: c>=lambda implies c(c-2)>=1>beta_word.
Induction on j then gives

    1+S_(j+1)=2(1+S_j)+a_word*c^j<=c^(j+1),
    S=S_m<c^m<3^m.                                 (38)

Also n=2^m-1>=2^(m-1), and for m>=10 the exact integer comparison
3^10<4^8, followed by multiplying the left/right by3/4 at each
increment, gives 3^m<4^(m-2)<=n^2/4. Thus
S<=n^2/4<=d_ff^2/4. The remaining conditions S>=2,d_ff>=4 hold
already for m>=3. In particular ALL of(34) hold for m>=1536.

At the actual n=2^m-1, use ONLY the accepted comparison(19):

    V_inc(H_m)/U_n^ZJ=B_n*V_inc(H_m)
                           <B_n*H_base(H_m)<237/250. (39)

No finite-field expectation, remote-cycle deletion or Gram-positivity
guess is a premise. Successful realizations would be static by(8), with
true radius1 under the declared strict condition; even such realizations
cannot improve the benchmark here. This closes ONLY law(24), its stated
coefficient range and its origin-anchored radius convention. It does not
exclude arbitrary signed incidence weights, nonlinear longitudinal
profiles, different initial anchors or terminal longitudinal reversals.

## 5. Law3: a fully legal changing-gate function with a terminal sign change

### 5.1 Static model, sharp smoothness and true radius

For delta_gate>0 define the symmetric Huber scalar

    Hub_delta(t)=t^2/2                    if |t|<=delta_gate,
                 delta_gate*|t|-delta_gate^2/2 otherwise,
    clip_delta(t)=max(-delta_gate,min(delta_gate,t)).

The ONE static function on R^2 is

    F_gate(x,y)=x^2/3+Hub_delta(x+y)/4.              (40)

Its gradient is

    grad F_gate(x,y)=(2x/3+clip_delta(x+y)/4,
                                      clip_delta(x+y)/4). (41)

The function is finite, differentiable and convex. It vanishes exactly
at x=0,x+y=0, so its unique minimum is(0,0). Start at
x0_gate=(0,2delta_gate), whose TRUE closest radius is2delta_gate.

Let e_x=(1,0), a_gate=(1,1), lambda_gate=2/3, kappa_gate=1/4.
The global Hessian upper matrix is

    M_gate=lambda_gate*e_x*e_x^T+kappa_gate*a_gate*a_gate^T
                       =[[11/12,1/4],[1/4,1/4]],
    I-M_gate=[[1/12,-1/4],[-1/4,3/4]]>=0.           (42)

The last matrix has nonnegative diagonal entries and determinant0.
For two points the derivative difference is obtained by integrating
matrices lambda_gate e_x e_x^T+theta kappa_gate a_gate a_gate^T,
with theta in[0,1], along the connecting segment. Each such matrix is
PSD and bounded by I, so grad F_gate is1-Lipschitz.
Near0 the Hessian is exactly M_gate, whose eigenvalues are1 and1/6:
its trace is7/6, its determinant1/6, and I-M_gate is singular.
Therefore1 is the SHARP global smoothness. No loose normalization is
used to create the finite gain.

### 5.2 Arbitrary words, complete data and ALL original cycles

For arbitrary h>=0 the actual full physical trajectory is defined by

    c_i=clip_delta(x_i+y_i),
    x_(i+1)=(1-2h_i/3)*x_i-(h_i/4)*c_i,
    y_(i+1)=y_i-(h_i/4)*c_i,
    g_i=(2x_i/3+c_i/4,c_i/4),
    f_i=x_i^2/3+Hub_delta(x_i+y_i)/4,               (43)

including the actual terminal g_n,f_n. This is the exact law of ONE
fixed function(40), not a different Hessian or oracle chosen at each
step. The equations are symbolic definitions, not executed recurrences.
Every zero step simply repeats the whole jet.

There is an exact ALL-pair cost formula. For any two samples i,j,
including the optimum jet, put p_i=x_i+y_i, d_x=x_j-x_i,
d_c=c_j-c_i, and

    B_Hub(i,j)=Hub_delta(p_j)-Hub_delta(p_i)-c_i*(p_j-p_i).

Direct substitution in the original orientation(3) gives

    Q_ij=-(lambda_gate/2)*d_x^2-kappa_gate*B_Hub(i,j)
             +||lambda_gate*d_x*e_x+kappa_gate*d_c*a_gate||^2/2. (44)

The scalar clip is nondecreasing1-Lipschitz. For p_j>p_i its Bregman
area is integral_(p_i)^(p_j)(clip_delta(t)-clip_delta(p_i))dt.
On the final subinterval of length d_c, monotonicity and Lipschitz1
bound that area below by the triangle area d_c^2/2. Reversing the
ordered endpoints uses the initial subinterval and gives the same bound.
Hence B_Hub(i,j)>=d_c^2/2 for every ordered pair.

Let A_gate have columns sqrt(lambda_gate)e_x and sqrt(kappa_gate)a_gate.
Because A_gate*A_gate^T=M_gate<=I, its operator norm is at most1.
Applying it to (sqrt(lambda_gate)d_x,sqrt(kappa_gate)d_c) gives

    ||lambda_gate*d_x*e_x+kappa_gate*d_c*a_gate||^2
                         <=lambda_gate*d_x^2+kappa_gate*d_c^2.

Thus(44) proves Q_ij<=0 for EVERY original directed pair and BOTH
optimum directions, with no finite-row omission. Summing(44) gives the
exact cost of any directed cycle and shows it is nonpositive.
The full Gram is automatically PSD from these actual R^2 vectors.
The true radius is already known from the unique minimum, not chosen
after examining a formal Gram.

### 5.3 A finite bridge which genuinely leaves the old sign restriction

Take h=(3/2,12,3/2), whose actual length is n=3. All coordinates and
gradients in the following table are divided by delta_gate; values are
divided by delta_gate^2.

| i | position (x_i,y_i)/delta_gate | (x_i+y_i)/delta_gate | g_i/delta_gate | f_i/delta_gate^2 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | (0,2) | 2 | (1/4,1/4) | 3/8 |
| 1 | (-3/8,13/8) | 5/4 | (0,1/4) | 15/64 |
| 2 | (-3/8,-11/8) | -7/4 | (-1/2,-1/4) | 23/64 |
| 3 | (3/8,-1) | -5/8 | (3/32,-5/32) | 49/512 |

The exact substitutions in(41),(43) verify every entry. Queries0,1
are on the positive plateau, query2 on the negative plateau, and the
terminal is inside the quadratic region. The background Hessian
diag(2/3,0) and M_gate do not commute: their off-diagonal products in
the two orders are different. Thus the active Hessian law changes.

In particular

    <g_3,x0_gate>=-5delta_gate^2/16<0,
    <g_0,g_3>=-delta_gate^2/64<0.                    (45)

The initial gradient is not aligned with x0_gate. The terminal component
along the initial-radius direction is NEGATIVE. This is outside the
positive-terminal/aligned-gradient obstruction of round59.

Its exact normalized true-radius payoff is

    V_gate=2F_gate(x_3,y_3)/(4delta_gate^2)=49/1024,
    H_base(h)=1/(1+2*(3/2+12+3/2))=1/31,
    V_gate/H_base=1519/1024>1.                       (46)

This is finite nonzero amplitude, exact values and ALL original rows.
No expectation or formal negative matrix direction is being called a
lower witness.

For the ORIGINAL actual-n benchmark, the exact ratio is

    V_gate/U_3^ZJ=49B_3/1024.                        (47)

It is below1. Here is a symbolic bound using the original all-split
definitions from acceptedb98bb807d514e80d, without executing a DP.
Let Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy))/2 and
G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2. Then C0=B0=1,
C1=lambda<5/2, B1=4. Monotonicity gives

    C2=Omega(1,lambda)<Omega(1,5/2)<9/2,
    B2=max{G(1,4),G(lambda,1)}<8.

The C2 bound follows from sqrt(89)<11. The B2 bounds follow from
sqrt(3)<2 and sqrt(21)<5. At horizon3 the THREE original split
expressions satisfy

    G(1,B2)<G(1,8)=6+4sqrt(2)<12,
    G(C1,B1)<G(5/2,4)=7+2sqrt(6)<12,
    G(C2,1)<G(9/2,1)=19/2+sqrt(37)/2<13.

Therefore B3<13 and the exact ratio(47) is <637/1024<1.
This is only a finite bridge beyond Huber. It is also below the accepted
same-word scalar single-cut witness: at the middle step P=T=3/2,
the scalar cut from factb98bb807d514e80d is121/25, which is larger
than49/1024. No superiority over the existing full witness portfolio
is claimed.

### 5.4 An exact arbitrary-horizon counterexample to this fixed law family

The legal word h_ann=(3/2,5,1,6) has actual length4. For EVERY
delta_gate>0 the SAME function family(40) and initialization yield
the following exact trajectory, divided by delta_gate:

| i | position | p_i | gradient |
|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | (0,2) | 2 | (1/4,1/4) |
| 1 | (-3/8,13/8) | 5/4 | (0,1/4) |
| 2 | (-3/8,3/8) | 0 | (-1/4,0) |
| 3 | (-1/8,3/8) | 1/4 | (-1/48,1/16) |
| 4 | (0,0) | 0 | (0,0) |

The first two steps bring the trajectory into the core. The remaining
steps1 and6 annihilate the two eigenmodes of M_gate; the displayed
intermediate point remains in the core, so this eigenmode explanation
is backed by the actual nonlinear gate checks, not just a matrix product.

The values at i=2,3,4, divided by delta_gate^2, are respectively
3/64,5/384,0. All original rows hold by(44). The true radius is still
2delta_gate and the terminal payoff is EXACTLY0.

For every n>=4 append n-4 zero steps. The actual iterate remains the
minimum, so the whole fixed scale family has

    sup_(delta_gate>0) V_gate(h_ann,0,...,0)=0,
    [sup_delta V_gate]/U_n^ZJ=0.                    (48)

The denominator is the original positive U_n^ZJ at THAT n. This is an
all-horizon family counterexample, not a computed finite trend.
It refutes using(40) with scale selection alone as the required all-word
adversary. It does not say W_n(h_ann,0,...,0)=0: other legal functions,
including the accepted scalar witnesses, have positive error there.
Varying the geometry, Hessian spectrum or initialization with h is
outside this fixed law family and remains open.

## 6. Original target interface: what remains unpaid

For every sufficiently large actual horizon n and EVERY predetermined
h>=0, the original affirmative target still requires a SINGLE legal
static function, finite dimension and attained minimum giving

    2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x0-x_star||^2)
                            >=(1-epsilon)/(2S_bullet(n)+1),

with a threshold in n independent of h. No statement above establishes
this. A negative answer still requires an actual full-class objective
UPPER bound with a fixed relative benchmark gap on unbounded horizons;
none is asserted here either.

The next decisive interface cannot merely be a covariance matrix whose
terminal row has the right sign. Laws1 and2 show exact original-row
payments that survive changes of scale and common values. Law3 shows
that genuine queried gate changes and terminal reversals are feasible,
but its two spectral modes are killed by a four-step word. A new law
would need word-dependent nonlinear memory/geometry which pays those
payments and avoids an annihilator, together with a uniform original
benchmark comparison. That final all-word comparison is an unpaid
THEOREM-LEVEL gap, not a technical lemma or a solved reduction.

No universal Huber optimality, covariance impossibility, unrestricted
W upper bound, resolution of(P), project finalization, revocation,
publication, shutdown or Goal completion is claimed.

## 7. Retrieval, writing and resource provenance

Read full TASK.md and PROBLEM.md, current elaboration/master_guidance,
accepted1252b8823bb5a290 and0e7e78056dc6258c in full, the complete
round59 finding6d0784199ba9ce6f, accepted5fd396eba75651e7 Section10,
and the statement/proof of acceptedb98bb807d514e80d. Targeted gm_search
recalled correlated Markov, remote-stage cycle, fixed queried-ridge,
overlapping directed-triangle and conditional-kernel failures before
selecting the laws. Those unverified findings were guidance only.

The query-memory, search-math-results, direct-proving and
construct-counterexamples skills guided the round. Matlas interpolation,
correlated-ridge and projective-incidence queries returned necessary
descent inequalities, unrelated algorithms, projective code minimal
weights and determinant conditions. None was a new proof premise.
Primary web retrieval identified the exact Taylor--Hendrickx--Glineur
interpolation source, arXiv:1502.05666. The already local exact extracted
paper was read at Theorem4 and its proof, Remark2 and Corollary1.
Its F_(0,1) terminology agrees with the original orientation(3) after
reversing indices. The actual interpolation premise here is the
self-contained accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290, not a newly imported theorem.

No extra finite-geometry machinery was needed: the proposed incidence
branch reduced to the already familiar real regular-simplex Gram, with
all field counts and normalization explicitly proved in Section4.1.
The new analytical ingredient is the all-history moment/first-cycle
payment(29)--(36), not a literature theorem or assumed orthogonality.

All new results remain UNVERIFIED because TASK.md prohibits formal
submission. Resources: mathematical programs0; numerical evaluations0;
arithmetic replays0; solvers0; executed trajectories/recurrences0;
benchmark DP0; fact_submit0; formal gates0; installations0; consult0;

## 8. Glossary

h,S,P_i,q_i,n are the full physical word, its mass, prefix masses,
normalized elapsed masses and ACTUAL horizon in(1). x_i,g_i,f_i,star
are the original sampled jets. Q_ij is(3). D_i^nc,C_i^val,tau_val,

The p_t,e,f in(31) are local normalized step moments, not field elements

# max75 nonlocal terminal-flow handoff

Completed the ONE assigned analytical invocation's three-law research work.
All new mathematics is UNVERIFIED, every mathematical shared finding has
fact_id=null, and no formal gate was called. The original goal is OPEN.
Actual outer-loop completion/counters/PID/exit are not observed or claimed.
No additional invocation, fourth law or verification slot is authorized.

Full report: joint_terminal_reroute_screens.md,879lines,39682bytes.
It is saved in this worker's research/nonlocal_terminal_flow_20261002/ and
the project research/nonlocal_terminal_flow_20261002/ directory. Its full
shared evidence is finding9161eec3956e6969. The companion glossary has74
manuscript-specific entries. All computations in the report are TEXTUAL
symbolic derivations, not executed mathematical or arithmetic programs.

## Constructed interface

For the actual unchanged enclosing-m tau1/4 word H_m, the complete
base coefficients are explicit finite dyadic sums/products. Its terminal
probabilities are alpha_star=product theta_t and
alpha_(n_m-2^(l-1))=s_(l-1)product_(t<l-1)theta_t.
With rhoRer=epsilon/4, each law samples an intermediate ORIGINAL query i
with a fully declared probability pi_i and splits every old n->j edge
through n->i->j. All terminal probabilities and every prefix row are
given in equation(16); each decrement is paid by that same terminal row,
all final signs are nonnegative, and full value flow is exact.

The complete original polynomial is the bilinear triangle action
DeltaM=2rhoRer sum_i,j pi_i alpha_j<g_n-g_i,y_i-y_j>.
Incoming optimum rows are explicitly zero and outgoing optimum weights
remain unchanged. Whole-word Huber/common slack, the complete initial
mixed charge and every transverse contact-defect expense are derived in
equations(2)–(8),(18)–(20), including the exact terminal-square remainder.
None is presumed PSD or used as an accepted intermediate result.

## Three scoped outcomes

1. Uniform queries, pi_i=1/n: explicit old H4 vector u=1,v0=0,w=-1
   gives added energy4(1-d_2)<0. A separate actual root right-first
   diagonal is negative for every enclosing m>=1536 and every q.
   Complete scoped finding e66de81a517b1e1d.

2. Physical step mass, pi_i=h_i/S_m: the entire actual root
   bridge/right-first gradient plane passes. The old H4 vector instead
   has energy2Wloc-[2b^2+(2-epsilon)^2+(1+d_2)^2]<0; together with
   its negative base it defeats every q and actual m>=1536.
   Complete scoped finding18deb80e726bbbcc.

3. Physical mass plus fixed residue subsidy,
   pi_i proportional h_i+65536*1_(i congruent3 mod4): the ENTIRE old H4
   two-dimensional plane has D_m M_C>3069epsilon normN, and the entire
   actual root bridge/right-first gradient plane is positive definite.
   Nevertheless j=m-3 has an ORIGINAL four-block vector(1,0,-1,0),
   with block length2^j, zero total displacement, initial and terminal.
   Its exact total action is
   D_m M_C=P_big+(2rhoRer Tmass D_m/Z_C)Q_big, P_big<0,
   Q_big<-(3/20)A_j^2. Thus D_m M_C<-(9/80)epsilon A_(m-3)^2
   for every actual m>=1536 and every real q.
   Complete scoped finding37353de9815ac111.

The last result pays both requested early screens and still fails the

# Three explicit nonlocal terminal transports and their original-space failures

JOINT ANALYTICAL DRAFT — UNVERIFIED. Worker max, invocation75 after
assigned ended baseline74, ended ceiling75, attempted ceiling76, cap1.
This manuscript is shared research evidence, not a fact. No formal gate,
mathematical program, arithmetic replay, executed trajectory/recurrence,
optimizer, installation, consult or subagent was used. Actual controller
completion and counters are not observed here.

The three laws change the terminal outgoing probability on EVERY original
finite prefix query, and add a completely specified coupled remote prefix
flow. They are constructed arrays, not selections from an unknown feasible
set. The uniform and physical-mass laws fail an old innovation direction.
The subsidized law passes the ENTIRE old two-dimensional innovation plane
and the actual root bridge/right-first gradient plane, but fails a larger
original dyadic innovation. Each failure is at the same physical enclosing
m, at every real objective level q. None is a W bound or a primal witness.

## 1. Target, actual word and accepted inputs

For a nonnegative word h of length n, W_n(h) is the supremum of
2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L||x_0-x_star||^2), over all finite-dimensional
Euclidean spaces, convex differentiable functions with L-Lipschitz
gradient and a minimizer x_star, L>0, x_0!=x_star, under
x_(i+1)=x_i-(h_i/L)grad f(x_i). This is W=2R.
The original benchmark, with every integer split retained, is

    C_0=B_0=1,
    Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy))/2,
    G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2,
    C_n=max_(0<=k<n)Omega(C_k,C_(n-k-1)),
    B_n=max_(0<=k<n)G(C_k,B_(n-k-1)), U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.

Fix ONE enclosing integer m>=1536, and throughout put

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,
    c=lambda(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j),
    n_j=2^j-1, S_j=sum H_j, A_j=1+S_j,
    d_j=a*c^j, D_j=2A_j-1, t_j=d_j/A_j, t_star=1/lambda.

All children use these SAME enclosing parameters. The actual diagnostic
word is h=H_m=H_(m,1/4); its physical entries are not altered or reordered.
Epsilon=0 will be a Silver calibration only. An identity on child H_j
always uses the original enclosing m, never epsilon=1/(4j).

Accepted inputs actually read are fact1252b8823bb5a290 in full (original
interpolation, every formal vector, and exact terminal-square theorem),
fact04219554e41d9750 Sections10–11 (primitive and objective conventions),
and fact5fd396eba75651e7 Section10 for ONLY

    B_(n_m)/D_m<237/250, m>=1536.

A full all-function upper W_(n_m)(H_m)<=zeta_m/D_m with
sup_m zeta_m<250/237 would therefore give a fixed improvement on
unbounded horizons. The restricted-family optimum in the last accepted
fact supplies no such upper. No original target is established here.

The full shared unverified1e4b5e4fdcb5a60f and f10a0fd97ba2b881,
including the scoped failures f251f43c220abd77,57162242c42d77eb,
309b078dc5f686e6, were read. They are awareness, not predecessors.
Every new formula reused from that regime is derived in THIS joint draft.
Guidancec8160224c76ffea9 and elaborationd53d7eabce096c9c govern this round.

## 2. Original full matrix and exact expenditure, jointly derived

Normalize smoothness1 and x_star=g_star=f_star=0. Retain the independent
original formal vectors (x_0,g_0,...,g_n), including terminal, bridge,
and any zero-step coordinates. Put

    x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, Ppos_i=sum_(t<i)h_t,
    S=sum h, D=1+2S, h_n=0,
    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2.

The residual convention is exactly fact1252b8823bb5a290; every actual
legal transcript has Q_ij<=0. Let K_ij be the finite multipliers,
nu_i the i->star rows, xiCert_i the star->i rows, and K_ii=0.
Value cancellation is precisely

    sum_j K_ij-sum_j K_ji+nu_i-xiCert_i=2*1_(i=n).       (1)

After it, the entire certificate is

    M(q)=q||x_0||^2-2f_n+sum K_ij Q_ij
                  +sum nu_i Q_(i,star)+sum xiCert_i Q_(star,i)
        =q||x_0||^2-sum nu_i<x_0,g_i>+Cgrad(g).        (2)

With out_i=nu_i+sum_j K_ij, collecting each diagonal and each ordered
position term yields

    Cgrad_ii=out_i-1_(i=n)-h_i sum_(v>i)K_iv,
    2Cgrad_ij=-(K_ij+K_ji)-h_j sum_(v>j)K_iv
                           +h_i[nu_j+sum_(v<=i)K_jv], i<j. (3)

Indeed half the full incident mass at i is out_i-1_(i=n) by (1).
A forward position term contributes -h_i K_iv to its source diagonal.
For i<j the gradient-difference term is -(K_ij+K_ji); source i's
later destinations contribute -h_j sum_(v>j)K_iv, and all source j
destinations at or before i, together with its outgoing optimum row,
contribute the last term. This proves the complete matrix, including
the initial mixed row -nu/2 and all original off-diagonals.
For q>0, full PSD is equivalent to

    Cgrad-nu nu^T/(4q)>=0                              (4)

on the ENTIRE gradient space. A selected plane does not replace (4).

For completeness both optimum directions are priced before specializing
to the three laws. Let e be the all-ones gradient column, csum=Cgrad e,
X=sum xiCert_i, E=sum(1+Ppos_i)xiCert_i, and Hprice=D X-E.
Summing (1) gives sum nu_i=2+X. Multiplying it by Ppos_i and summing
gives sum K_ij(Ppos_i-Ppos_j)=2S-sum(nu_i-xiCert_i)Ppos_i.
Expansion at a common gradient then gives

    e^T Cgrad e=D+E,
    M(x_0=r,g=t e)=q r^2-(2+X)r t+(D+E)t^2.           (5)

The one-sided Huber function, zero for z<=0, z^2/2 on[0,1] and
z-1/2 above1, is convex and1-smooth. At x_0=D all samples lie above1:
g_i=1, x_i=D-Ppos_i, f_i=D-Ppos_i-1/2. Finite and outgoing optimum
residuals vanish; Q_(star,i)=-(2S-Ppos_i). At q=zeta/D this gives

    M(D,e)=(zeta-1)D-Hprice.                           (6)

Whole-word necessary prices are Hprice<=(zeta-1)D and
zeta>=D(2+X)^2/[4(D+E)]. These are single total allowances.
Completing the common-gradient/initial-point block, with v in e-perp,
gives its exact transverse charge. If

    DeltaCommon=q(D+E)-(2+X)^2/4>0,

the charge is

    Price(v)=[(D+E)(nu^T v)^2/4
       -(2+X)(nu^T v)(csum^T v)/2+q(csum^T v)^2]/DeltaCommon. (7)

This follows by inverting [[q,-(2+X)/2],[-(2+X)/2,D+E]] and
using the coupling (-nu^T v/2,csum^T v). In the singular case the
coupling must annihilate that block's kernel; no mode is discarded.

Our constructed laws have xiCert=0. They therefore have sum nu=2,
e^T Cgrad e=D, Hprice=0 and exact Huber slack(zeta-1)D. Set
dcontact=csum-D nu/2, so e^T dcontact=0. Equation(7) becomes

    Price(v)=(csum^T v)^2/D+(dcontact^T v)^2/[D(zeta-1)]. (8)

At the advertised zeta_0=101/100 the second term has coefficient100/D.
Equations(2)–(8) retain EVERY initial, common and transverse direction.
They are calculations in this joint UNVERIFIED draft, not accepted bricks.
Any negative direction at x_0=0 survives every inflation of q.

## 3. Fully explicit base flow; no PSD selection oracle

The base is an explicitly prescribed array. It is NOT asserted PSD on the
actual positive-epsilon word. A closed finite sum for all its coefficients
is supplied, together with identities establishing their signs and flow.

Finite summation of H_j gives

    v_h=a/(c-2),
    A_j=v_h c^j+(1-v_h)2^j,
    t_j=(c-2)/[1+(1/v_h-1)(2/c)^j].                   (9)

Here0<v_h<1 since c(c-2)>1>beta. The ratios increase to c-2.
In particular t_0,t_1<t_star<t_2, and for j>=2,

    0<t_j-t_star<c-lambda=lambda epsilon, t_j<1/2.     (10)

For the signs at j=1,2, direct physical substitution gives
lambda d_1-A_1=epsilon[-lambda+2/(lambda(1+epsilon))]<0 and
c(lambda d_2-A_2)=epsilon[(lambda+4)-(4lambda+2)epsilon
                                      -(5lambda+2)epsilon^2]>0.
The last bracket exceeds5 using12/5<lambda<5/2 and epsilon<=1/6144.

Define the complete primitive array Lambda^j on indices0..n_j.
At a join H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+d_j,H_j), with bridge p and terminal z,
embed the left array with weight1, the right with weight k_j, and add

    u_j h_i at i->p, v_j h_i at i->z for every right i<z,
    w_j at z->p, z_j at p->z,
    u_j=2lambda,
    v_j=[u_j d_j+2(A_j+d_j)]/A_j,
    k_j=1+(u_j+v_j)/2=2+lambda+(lambda+1)t_j,
    z_j=2A_j,
    w_j=4A_j+2d_j-u_j(A_j-1).                        (11)

Lambda^0=0. All unlisted rows are zero; repeated rows are summed.
This is also a closed finite coefficient formula: sum(11)'s four row
types over every dyadic internal node, multiplied by the product of
k_l over precisely those ancestors in whose RIGHT child the node lies.
Left ancestors contribute1. Thus each coefficient is a specified finite
sum of products of(9)–(11), with no unknown optimization or matrix choice.

Every coefficient is nonnegative. The first four scalar quantities are
positive. Also w_j=2lambda+2(d_j-A_j/lambda): j>=2 follows from(10),
j=0 has w_0=4+2a, and j=1 follows from A_1<lambda, giving w_1>2lambda-2.
The identities u_j+v_j=2(k_j-1) and
u_j(A_j-1)+w_j-z_j=2(A_j+d_j) give divergence -2h_i at each
nonterminal original query and2(A_(j+1)-1) at its terminal.

Put

    V_j=2sum_(i<n_j)h_i(f_i-f_(n_j))
       +sum_(i<n_j)h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2
       -A_j(A_j-1)||g_(n_j)||^2,
    Psrc_j=V_j+sum Lambda^j_iv Q_iv.

At one original join let r=sum_(i right,i<z)h_i g_i,
Y=r-(A_j-1)g_z and Z=g_p-g_z. Direct expansion yields

    Psrc_(j+1)=Psrc_j^left+k_j Psrc_j^right
       +(t_star-t_j)[(lambda+1)||Y||^2
          -2lambda A_j<Y,Z>+A_j^2(2+t_star+t_j)||Z||^2]. (12)

To audit all new terms, their four gradient polynomials before weighting
are, in(11)'s order,

    d_j<r,g_p>+||r||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2
                                      +(A_j-1)||g_p||^2/2;
    -||r||^2/2+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2/2
                                      +(A_j-1)||g_z||^2/2-<r,g_z>;
    d_j<g_z,g_p>+<g_z,r>+(||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2)/2;
    -(d_j+1/2)||g_p||^2+||g_z||^2/2-<g_p,r>-<g_p,g_z>.

Their r^2,r*g_p,r*g_z coefficients are(u_j-v_j)/2,
u_j d_j-z_j and w_j-v_j. Adding V_j and the child terms yields(12).
Its bracket is positive definite since its determinant divided by A_j^2
is(lambda+1)(2+t_star+t_j)-lambda^2>=lambda>0.
Equation(12) is an identity on ALL original gradients; at positive
epsilon it is not an induction that child matrices are PSD.

The full base objective array Kbase^j, mubase^j has empty base
Kbase^0=0, mubase^0_0=2, xiCert=0. At the same join write
A=A_j-1, bjoin=1+d_j, D=D_(j+1), D_c=D_j,
s_j=(A+bjoin)/(3A+2bjoin), theta_j=1-s_j. Put

    Kbase_(L_l,L_v)=Lambda^j_lv/D,
    Kbase_(R_i,R_v)=Kbase^j_iv,
    Kbase_(R_i,p)=s_j mubase^j_i,
    mubase_(L_l)=2h_l/D for l<n_j,
    mubase_p=2s_j-2A/D,
    mubase_(R_i)=theta_j mubase^j_i.                  (13)

All other finite rows and every incoming optimum row are zero.
Here L_l=l, p=L_(n_j), R_i=n_j+1+i, and R_(n_j) is the terminal.
For A=0, s_0=1/2; otherwise1/3<s_j<1/2. The bridge optimum row is
positive because(A+bjoin)D-A(3A+2bjoin)
=A^2+4A*bjoin+2bjoin^2+A+bjoin>0.
Primitive divergence is canceled by the left optimum rows; its terminal
divergence2A/D is canceled by the bridge optimum row and incoming right
mass2s_j. Each right node preserves flow since theta_j+s_j=1.
This proves(1), every final sign and sum mubase=2. No row enters the
unqueried actual terminal: this holds in the empty base and in every(13).

For an explicit unrolled original-index version at depth r, the right
spine child of depth l has offset o_l=2^r-2^l and bridge
p_l=n_r-2^(l-1), 1<=l<=r. Sum Lambda^(l-1)/D_l on its entire left
query block[o_l,p_l], and add s_(l-1)mubase^(l-1)_i at each original
right query i->p_l, INCLUDING the final query. These are every finite
row. Outgoing optimum rows on this left block are

    (2h_i/D_l) product_(t=l)^(r-1)theta_t for i<p_l,
    [2s_(l-1)-2(A_(l-1)-1)/D_l] product_(t=l)^(r-1)theta_t at p_l,

and at n_r they are2 product_(t=0)^(r-1)theta_t.
The same finite formula inside the child gives every mubase used in the
spine addition. All coefficients are thus closed finite products/sums.

Define astar_j=product_(t=0)^(j-1)theta_t, astar_0=1.
The ENTIRE outgoing terminal probability of this base is explicitly

    alpha_star^base=astar_r,
    alpha_(p_l)^base=s_(l-1)astar_(l-1), 1<=l<=r,
    alpha_i^base=0 at every other finite prefix index.  (14)

They sum to1 since s_j astar_j=astar_j-astar_(j+1).
There is no assumed optimum re-selection in(14).

Finally let r_R=sum_i mubase^j_i g_(R_i),
x_p=x_0-sum_(l<n_j)h_l g_(L_l),
Rchild_R=Cbase^j(g_R)-D_c||r_R||^2/4, and
kappa_j=D_c-D theta_j^2. The entire base identity is

    Mbase_(j+1)(1/D)=Psrc_j(g_L)/D+Rchild_R
       +||x_p+(A-D s_j)g_p-(D theta_j/2)r_R||^2/D
       +(kappa_j/4)||r_R-2g_p||^2.                   (15)

Here Cbase is the gradient matrix from(3) for(13). To check the
initial and every mixed term, the polynomial after source extraction is
||x_p||^2/D+2(A/D-s_j)<x_p,g_p>+(A^2/D+2s_j)||g_p||^2
-theta_j<x_p,r_R>+(bjoin-s_j)<g_p,r_R>+D_c||r_R||^2/4.
The scalar equality s_j(3A+2bjoin)=A+bjoin completes it to(15).
This also proves base contact Cbase e=D mubase/2: the primitive common
gradient derivatives vanish by(12), and the displayed squares vanish
at x_0=D,g_i=1 with the inherited child contact identity. Empty base
contact starts the induction. Neither Rchild_R nor kappa_j is presumed
nonnegative on the positive-epsilon word.

## 4. Constructed nonlocal terminal reroute and complete prefix flow

At actual depth r>=3 choose ONE of the explicit distributions below and
rhoRer=epsilon/4. For r<=2 set rhoRer=0. Write n=n_r, D=D_r,
alpha_i=alpha_i^base for i<n, astar=astar_r, Tmass=1-astar.
Let piRer_i be a probability on ALL finite original prefix indices0..n-1.
Define every FINAL row by

    alpha_star^new=astar,
    alpha_i^new=(1-rhoRer)alpha_i+rhoRer*Tmass*piRer_i,
    Knew_(n,i)=2alpha_i^new, nunew_n=2astar,
    Knew_(i,n)=0 for every i<n,
    Knew_(i,j)=Kbase_(i,j)+2rhoRer*piRer_i*alpha_j,
                         i,j<n, i!=j,
    nunew_i=mubase_i for i<n, xiCertnew_i=0.          (16)

All unlisted rows are zero. In particular the star is retained and has
no incoming direction in these laws, as explicitly chosen here.
Every row is nonnegative. The only decrement is rhoRer of each actual
original terminal finite edge, which is paid by that SAME edge.
No prefix row is decremented. The self-pair i=j is skipped; its path
keeps its old terminal edge rather than creating a diagonal residual.

The construction splits each old path n->j into n->i->j with probability
rhoRer*piRer_i. Prefix added divergence is exactly
2rhoRer(Tmass*piRer_i-alpha_i); the terminal incoming change at i is
the same quantity. Thus(1) holds at EVERY original finite node, and the
prefix flow of fact1252b8823bb5a290 is exactly out_i^pre-in_i^pre
=2alpha_i^new. Terminal outgoing mass is exactly2. This proves full
value flow without adopting a previous unverified flow identity.

For every original ordered triple, direct expansion gives

    Q_(n,i)+Q_(i,j)-Q_(n,j)=<g_n-g_i,y_i-y_j>,
    y_i=x_i-g_i.                                    (17)

The value terms cancel. The position terms are
<g_n-g_i,x_i-x_j>; the difference-square terms are
-<g_n-g_i,g_i-g_j>, proving(17) on the ENTIRE original formal space.
Consequently the full law is the explicit polynomial

    Mnew(q)=Mbase(q)+DeltaM,
    DeltaM=2rhoRer sum_(i,j<n)piRer_i alpha_j<g_n-g_i,y_i-y_j>.

Set ybar=sum_(j<n)alpha_j y_j and gbar=sum_(i<n)piRer_i g_i.
Expanding that finite sum, without assuming independence pays a square,

    DeltaM=2rhoRer[<g_n,Tmass sum_i piRer_i y_i-ybar>
                  -Tmass sum_i piRer_i<g_i,y_i>+<gbar,ybar>]. (18)

Equations(3),(13),(16) specify every coefficient, including every new
off-diagonal. Equation(18) is a bilinear identity, NOT a PSD claim.

The terminal-square fact applies to these concrete rows:

    V_new=x_n-astar*y_star-sum_i alpha_i^new y_i,
    R_new=q||x_0||^2-2sum_i alpha_i^new(f_i-||g_i||^2/2)
                 -||V_new||^2+sum_(i!=j in J_pre)lambda^pre_new_ij Q_ij,
    Mnew=||g_n-V_new||^2+R_new.                       (19)

Here y_star=0 and lambda^pre_new consists EXACTLY of(16)'s prefix K
and nu, with zero incoming optimum rows. This is the complete original
prefix remainder, not an oracle or a conditional-covariance certificate.
Its cumulative terminal probabilities w_t are the finite sums of(16),
and V_new=astar*x_0-sum_(t<n)(h_t w_t-alpha_t^new)g_t as in the
accepted fact. All original gradients remain independent.

Initial mixed coefficients nu are unchanged. The contact defect is
nonzero in general and paid, not suppressed. Define
Pbar=sum_(j<n)alpha_j Ppos_j, Ppi=sum_i piRer_i Ppos_i. Differentiating
(18) at a common gradient, where g_n-g_i=0 and y_i-y_j=Ppos_j-Ppos_i,
gives the ENTIRE changed defect

    dcontact_i=rhoRer*piRer_i(Tmass*Ppos_i-Pbar), i<n,
    dcontact_n=rhoRer(Pbar-Tmass*Ppi).                 (20)

The components sum to zero. Base contact was proved after(15), so(20)
is the full defect, not just one contribution. Substitute csum=D nu/2
+dcontact into(8): every transverse vector pays the exact square(20),
with coefficient1/[D(zeta-1)], in addition to the complete common
charge. Huber and common mixed prices are unchanged: Hprice=0 and the
common block is [[zeta/D,-1],[-1,D]], with determinant zeta-1.
The initial-point Schur charge for every original gradient is still
nu nu^T/(4q), but its gradient matrix is now the FULL(18).
No renewable local objective allowance is used.

These formulas are polynomial identities even with zero physical steps;
they impose no equality on repeated formal gradients. Empty r=0 is the
accepted direct certificate lambda_(0,star)=2, M=||x_0-g_0||^2.
No bound is claimed for a different word or a compressed horizon.

## 5. Three concrete laws and full small/Silver calibrations

For a nonempty depth-r word, define the following CLOSED distributions:

    Law A: piRer_i=1/n_r, Z_A=n_r.

    Law B: piRer_i=h_i/S_r, Z_B=S_r.

    Law C: Lmask=65536,
           vmask_i=h_i+Lmask*1_(i congruent3 modulo4),
           Z_C=S_r+Lmask*floor(n_r/4),
           piRer_i=vmask_i/Z_C.                     (21)

For r>=2, floor(n_r/4)=2^(r-2)-1. All three distributions sum to1.
The actual H_r has positive steps, so all three give strictly positive
new alpha_i at EVERY finite prefix query when r>=3. Their star probability
is precisely astar_r. Law C fixes its mask and coefficient once; it does
not solve for a feasible distribution, use a running budget, or retune
the old finite cycles or adjacent mixture. Every original prefix edge
is declared by(16), including remote edges from each i to EVERY nonzero
base terminal destination p_l. The construction has actual remote support.

At r<=2, rhoRer=0, so the complete actual H2 calibration is the base,
with ALL original initial mixed terms. Put b=1+a; then7/5<b<sqrt(2)<10/7.
At H1, (11) and(13) give

    Psrc_1=(2-b^2)||g_0-g_1||^2,
    Mbase_1(1/D_1)=||x_0-D_1(g_0+g_1)/2||^2/D_1
                              +(3-2b)||g_0-g_1||^2/4. (22)

Both remainders are PSD. At H2 the physical bridge is2-epsilon,
D_1>19/5, D_2<75/7, and
theta_1=(2b+2-epsilon)/(3b+4-2epsilon)<59/100.
The last ratio increases in b and decreases in the physical bridge;
use b<10/7 and2-epsilon>199/100 to prove the bound by cross multiplication.
Then D_2 theta_1^2<15/4 and kappa_1>1/20.
The full(15), its entire H1 Schur remainder from(22), and Psrc_1 prove
full H2 PSD. These are original-vector calculations, not quotient tests.

At epsilon=0 every rhoRer is zero. Also t_j=t_star and Psrc_j=0 by(12).
Writing u_star=1/lambda, A=A_j-1 and bjoin=1+u_star(A+1), expansion gives

    (3A+2bjoin)^2*kappa_j
      =2(1-u_star)A^3+(6-8u_star)A^2
       +(1+u_star)(8u_star-2)A+(1+u_star)^2(1-2u_star)>0. (23)

Each coefficient is positive since1/4<u_star<1/2. Starting with the
empty square, (15) and base contact give full Silver PSD by induction:
the ENTIRE child Schur remainder is PSD once its full matrix is PSD.
This use of child PSD is confined to the proved epsilon=0 calibration.
No positive-epsilon child PSD is assumed anywhere below.

## 6. Original old H4 plane and exact all-depth base restriction

Write Wloc=A_2+d_2, Uloc=A_2+d_3, rloc=Uloc/Wloc,
Edef=(lambda d_2-A_2)(A_2+d_2/lambda)>0 and delta3=t_3-t_star>0.
Choose the one-dimensional ORIGINAL formal-vector test

    g_0,...,g_3=u, g_4,...,g_7=v0,
    g_8,...,g_11=w, g_12,...,g_15=0,
    Wloc(u+w)+Uloc*v0=0.                            (24)

Let N be this two-dimensional zero-displacement plane; it chooses values
of independent test vectors and identifies no formal coordinates.
Equation(12) at its three actual joins gives EXACTLY

    P_N=-Edef(u-v0)^2-k_3 Edef*w^2
       -delta3[(lambda+1)Wloc^2*w^2
          -2lambda A_3 Wloc*w*v0+A_3^2(2+t_star+t_3)*v0^2]<0 (25)

at every nonzero point of N. The left H3 has Y=0,Z=u-v0;
the right H3 contributes -Edef*w^2. The final bracket is positive
definite by(12), so strict negativity follows also when either part vanishes.

Place(24) in the FIRST actual H4 of H_m, set x_0=0 and EVERY other
original gradient, INCLUDING g_n and the root bridge, to zero. Its zero
terminal and displacement make every later source Y,Z zero on the all-left
embedding, whose weight is1. The final(15) has zero boundary and right
variables. Hence, for every m>=5 and every real q,

    D_m Mbase_m(q)|N=P_N.                            (26)

Every base terminal destination p_l lies after this active block when
m>=6. Its gradient and position are zero. Therefore ybar=0 on N, and
(18) gives the exact whole-word restriction for EACH distribution

    D_m Mnew(q)|N=P_N+(2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/Z)*Q_weight,
    Q_weight=sum_(i=0)^11 weight_i(g_i^2-g_i*x_i),     (27)

where weight_i is1,h_i, or vmask_i, and Z is the corresponding(21).
No child allowance or omitted terminal term appears in(27).

For physical weights and any zero-displacement test,

    sum h_i(g_i^2-g_i*x_i)=sum h_i(1-h_i/2)g_i^2      (28)

at x_0=0. Indeed sum h_i g_i=0, so
sum_(t<i)h_i h_t g_i g_t=-(sum h_i^2 g_i^2)/2.
This is an exact identity in the selected original vector, not a numerical
trajectory or an assumption about legal sampled values.

## 7. Laws A and B: early exact failures

Use u=1,v0=0,w=-1 in(24). It has zero displacement and every retained
optimum, terminal and inherited initial contribution already included.
Within each active four-query block the local prefixes are
0,b,b+2-epsilon,2b+2-epsilon, with total physical displacement Wloc.
For Law A this gives

    Q_uniform=8+2[4b+2(2-epsilon)]-4Wloc=4(1-d_2)<0. (29)

For Law B, (28) gives

    Q_physical=2Wloc-[2b^2+(2-epsilon)^2+(1+d_2)^2]<0. (30)

For example Wloc<826/100, b>7/5, 2-epsilon>199/100, d_2>12/5
give Q_physical<1652/100-98/25-39601/10000-289/25<-29/10.
The elementary parameter bounds are proved in Section9 below.
Combining(25),(27),(29),(30), BOTH complete laws fail on this original
direction for every real q and every actual m>=1536. All signs and
value flows remain valid; the gradient matrix does not.

For Law A the negative added curvature is exactly
(8rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/n_m)(1-d_2) after D_m normalization.
For Law B it is exactly(2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/S_m)Q_physical.
Thus the actual whole-word cost is displayed, without reassigning it to
a new local allowance. Section10 also supplies the separate actual root
screen: Law A loses the right-first diagonal; Law B passes that two-query
plane but its independent early failure(30) persists.

## 8. Quantitative growth and base root entries without child PSD

We need explicit original-coordinate root entries to screen Law C before
the long lift. No child PSD estimate is used. From c(c-2)
=1+2(lambda+1)epsilon+lambda^2 epsilon^2 and lambda<5/2,

    v_h=(1-epsilon)/[c(c-2)]>1-9epsilon>255/256.       (31)

For the first inequality compare with1+7epsilon+7epsilon^2;
(1-9epsilon)(1+7epsilon+7epsilon^2)
=1-2epsilon-56epsilon^2-63epsilon^3<1-epsilon.
For every j>=1533, the third binomial term gives

    A_j/2^j>(255/256)(6/5)^j
       >(255/256) j(j-1)(j-2)/750>4000000.           (32)

Here j,j-1,j-2>1500, and1500^3/750=4500000.
In particular S_m>1000000*2^m. These are symbolic inequalities, not
an executed recurrence, scalar replay, or benchmark computation.

Let pterm_j denote Psrc_j with only its terminal gradient equal1.
Substituting Y=-(A_j-1),Z=-1 into(12) gives

    pterm_(j+1)=k_j pterm_j+(t_star-t_j)F_j,
    F_j=A_j^2(1+t_j)-2A_j+(lambda+1), pterm_0=0.      (33)

It can be read as the CLOSED finite sum
pterm_j=sum_(l<j)(t_star-t_l)F_l product_(v=l+1)^(j-1)k_v.
No value of that recurrence is executed. Since F_j=(A_j-1)^2+t_j A_j^2
+lambda>0, pterm_1,pterm_2>=0. For j>=2,

    k_j-(2+t_j)^2=(t_star-t_j)(1+t_j)<0,
    0<F_j/A_(j+1)^2<=(1+t_j)/(2+t_j)^2<=1/4.          (34)

The middle bound uses A_j>(lambda+1)/2. Therefore the normalized value
can decrease by at most lambda epsilon/4 at each later level: if it is
negative, multiplication by k_j/(2+t_j)^2 in(0,1) makes it larger;
if positive, the product remains positive. This proves for j<=m-1

    pterm_j>=-j lambda epsilon*A_j^2/4>-5A_j^2/32.    (35)

This is a scalar diagonal LOWER estimate from an exact identity, not a
claim that the source or any child is PSD.

Set j=m-1, Bmass=A_j, p=n_j and qright=p+1. The physical root bridge
is h_p=1+d_j; the right-first step is b=1+a. Define astarch=astar_j,
ap=s_j astarch, T_R=1-astarch, Tmass=ap+T_R=1-theta_j astarch.
The base terminal destinations consist of p with mass ap and right
destinations with total T_R. None is qright at these depths.
Since theta_l<=2/3, astarch<1/100 (use j>=12 and (2/3)^12<1/100).

Formula(3), with the fully expanded base(13), gives EXACTLY

    Cbase_pp=2s_j+[(Bmass-1)^2+pterm_j]/D_m,
    Cbase_qright,qright=2/D_j,
    Cbase_p,qright=b(h_p-s_j)/D_j.                   (36)

For the first entry the outgoing primitive mass at its terminal is
A_j^2-1+pterm_j, as follows from the terminal diagonal in V_j and(3).
The root right-first diagonal and mixed term follow from its inherited
child row, split optimum weight theta_j*mubase_0, backward weight
s_j*mubase_0 and mubase_0=2b/D_j. All inherited prefixes are retained.

By(10), d_j in((2/5)Bmass,Bmass/2), D_m<5Bmass,
and Bmass>=2^j>1250. Equations(35),(36) then give

    Cbase_pp>Bmass/6,
    Cbase_qright,qright>1/Bmass,
    7/25<Cbase_p,qright<2/5.                         (37)

For the first, ((27/32)Bmass^2-2Bmass+1)/(5Bmass)>Bmass/6
when Bmass>1250. For the mixed upper use b<10/7 and h_p<1+Bmass/2;
the lower follows from b>7/5 and d_j>(2/5)Bmass.
Thus the whole root two-query GRADIENT plane, with x_0=g_n=0 and every
other gradient zero, is positive definite already for the prescribed
base, even though its other directions are indefinite.

## 9. Law C passes the entire old N plane at the actual word

The fixed subsidy is large enough to pay this screen, with no allowance
renewed at higher levels. Let normN=u^2+v0^2+w^2 on(24).
On a zero-displacement N vector, define Q_mask using unit weights only
at original queries3,7,11; query15 has zero gradient. Since
x_3=-(A_2-1)u, x_7=-Wloc*u-(A_2-1)v0,
x_11=-Wloc*u-Uloc*v0-(A_2-1)w,

    Q_mask=A_2(u^2+v0^2+w^2)+Wloc*u(v0+w)+Uloc*v0*w.

Using u=-w-rloc*v0,

    Q_mask=(A_2-d_2)w^2+(2A_2 rloc-Wloc)w*v0
                                +(A_2 rloc^2-d_3)v0^2. (38)

We prove a uniform lower bound, rather than checking one lucky point.
The actual epsilon<=1/6144 implies

    582/100<A_2<584/100, 241/100<d_2<242/100,
    582/100<d_3<584/100, 141/100<rloc<142/100.          (39)

For explicit verification use707/500<sqrt(2)<283/200 and
b=sqrt(2)-2epsilon/[lambda(1+epsilon)] to get
5827/1000<A_2=3+2b-epsilon<583/100.
Also d_2=lambda(1-epsilon^2) lies in(2413/1000,2415/1000), and
d_3=lambda^2(1-epsilon^2)(1+epsilon) lies in(5827/1000,584/100).
These sharper intervals give
rloc>(2*5827/1000)/(583/100+2415/1000)>141/100 and
rloc<(583/100+584/100)/(5827/1000+2413/1000)<142/100.

Subtract normN/8= w^2/4+rloc*w*v0/4+(rloc^2+1)v0^2/8
from(38). Its w^2 coefficient exceeds63/20; its v0^2 coefficient
exceeds53/10; the absolute value of its cross coefficient is less81/10.
These follow by direct insertion of(39):

    A_2-d_2-1/4>5.82-2.42-.25=3.15,
    A_2 rloc^2-d_3-(rloc^2+1)/8
       >5.82*(1.41)^2-5.84-[(1.42)^2+1]/8>5.3,
    0<2A_2 rloc-Wloc-rloc/4<8.1.

The determinant is positive because
(63/20)(53/10)=3339/200>6561/400=(81/10)^2/4.
Thus the entire plane has

    Q_mask>normN/8.                                 (40)

The strict inequality in(40) is for nonzero N vectors; both sides are
zero at the zero vector. The strict bounds in(41) below have the same
nonzero-vector convention.

The physical part Q_physical from(28) is greater than -100normN:
each four-query block has steps below7, so the sum of its four negative
terms -h_i^2/2 exceeds -98; positive terms may be discarded.
The source base loss is also fully bounded. In(25), Edef<24epsilon,
k_3<7, delta3<(5/2)epsilon, Wloc<9, A_3<15,
lambda+1<4, 2+t_star+t_3<3. The absolute cross term is at most
lambda A_3 Wloc(w^2+v0^2)<338(w^2+v0^2).
Together these give

    P_N>-3000epsilon*normN,
    Q_vmask=Q_physical+65536Q_mask>8092normN.          (41)

By(32), Z_C=S_m+65536(2^(m-2)-1)<(33/32)S_m;
Tmass>99/100. Since D_m>2S_m and rhoRer=epsilon/4,

    2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/Z_C
       >(epsilon/2)(99/100)(64/33)>(3/4)epsilon.       (42)

Combining(27),(41),(42), at EVERY nonzero original N vector,

    D_m M_C(q)|N>3069epsilon*normN>0.                (43)

This pays the WHOLE old two-dimensional screen with the actual global
normalization, not a reset child normalizer. It does not assert full PSD.
In particular the large fixed subsidy may create additional isolated
query defects; all those entries remain explicitly in(3),(16),(18).
The exact deeper negative direction below is valid regardless of those
additional screens. No such direction is hidden or priced as free.

## 10. Actual root bridge/right-first screens for all three laws

Let omega=piRer_p and eta=piRer_qright. In the original two-query test
g_p=u_root, g_qright=v_root, x_0=g_n=0, all other gradients zero,
y_p=-u_root, y_qright=-h_p*u_root-v_root. From the COMPLETE base terminal
distribution(14),
ybar=-(ap+T_R h_p)u_root-T_R b*v_root.
Substitution into(18) gives exactly

    DeltaC_pp=2rhoRer*omega*T_R(1-h_p),
    DeltaC_qright,qright=2rhoRer*eta*(Tmass-b*T_R),
    DeltaC_p,qright=rhoRer[eta*ap*(h_p-1)-omega*T_R*b]. (44)

These include every remote destination and every inherited position.
They are not adjacent-only approximations.

For Law A, omega=eta=1/n_m, and

    Tmass-b*T_R=1-b+(b-theta_j)astarch<-(b-1)/2.

Use astarch<1/100, b>7/5 and b<3/2 for this strict bound.
Its actual right-first diagonal therefore satisfies

    C_A,qright,qright<2/D_j-rhoRer*(b-1)/n_m<0.       (45)

Here D_j/n_m>96m: D_j>(3/2)A_j and n_m<2^(j+1) give
D_j/n_m>(3v_h/4)(6/5)^(m-1).
The third binomial term is greater than m^3/6000>300m for m>=1536,
so the last expression exceeds200m. Then
rhoRer*(b-1)D_j/n_m>(1/(16m))*(2/5)*96m=12/5>2.
The ORIGINAL assignment g_qright=1, all other vectors zero, rejects Law A
at every q. It is a root right-first defect separate from(29).

For Law B, omega=h_p/S_m, eta=b/S_m. For Law C,
omega=(h_p+65536)/Z_C, eta=b/Z_C, since p is3 modulo4 and qright is0.
In both cases Bmass>128*65536, S_m>2Bmass and h_p<1+Bmass/2 give
omega<13/50, eta<=b/S_m. Equations(37),(44) imply

    C_new,pp>Bmass*(1/6-rhoRer/3),
    C_new,qright,qright>(1-rhoRer)/Bmass,
    7/25-rhoRer/2<C_new,p,qright<2/5.                (46)

For the first, the decrement is2rhoRer*omega*T_R*d_j<rhoRer*Bmass/3.
For the second use Tmass-b*T_R>=-(b-1), b(b-1)<1, and S_m>2Bmass.
For the mixed entry the correction is negative since ap<1/100,
T_R>99/100 and eta*ap*d_j<omega*T_R*b, and its magnitude is less
rhoRer*(13/50)*(10/7)<rhoRer/2.
Because rhoRer<1/1000, the determinant exceeds

    (1/6-1/3000)*(999/1000)-4/25
       =(498501-480000)/3000000>0.                  (47)

Thus Laws B AND C pass the ENTIRE actual root two-query gradient plane,
including the old direction g_p=1,g_qright=-Bmass/25. This is precisely
a screen with x_0=g_n=0. It is not a claim about the larger terminal
Schur plane, initial mixed directions, singular modes or full PSD.
All those directions continue to be represented by(3),(18),(19),(20).

## 11. Law C: exact full-depth failure after both screens pass

Set j=m-3>=1533 and Kblock=2^j. In the FIRST original H_(j+2)=H_(m-1),
choose the following original one-dimensional formal vector:

    g_i=1 for0<=i<Kblock,
    g_i=0 forKblock<=i<2Kblock,
    g_i=-1 for2Kblock<=i<3Kblock,
    g_i=0 for3Kblock<=i<=n_m,
    x_0=0.                                         (48)

Both active blocks contain the physical H_j followed by its actual
bridge1+d_j, so each has displacement Lblock=A_j+d_j, with opposite
sign. The entire prefix displacement is zero, its terminal query is zero,
and EVERY subsequent original query has position and gradient zero.
No gradients are deleted, no child parameters are reset, and (48) need
not be a legal primal transcript to test PSD of the full matrix.

Define E_j=(t_j-t_star)A_j^2(lambda+t_j)>0.
For the first H_(j+1), the all-common H_j source remainder is zero by(12),
the right child is zero, and its join has Y=0,Z=1. Thus its source value
is -E_j. The third-block copy similarly has source -E_j.
The H_(j+2) join has Y=-Lblock,Z=0. Hence the EXACT source value is

    P_big=-(1+k_(j+1))E_j
                   -(t_(j+1)-t_star)(lambda+1)Lblock^2<0. (49)

At the final objective join (48) kills all boundary/right terms in(15).
Every base terminal destination p_l is at or beyond the zero terminal
of this first H_(m-1), so ybar=0. For EVERY real q, (18) gives

    D_m M_C(q;48)=P_big+(2rhoRer*Tmass*D_m/Z_C)*Q_big. (50)

This is the whole original-space action including ALL remote prefix
rows, the optimum, terminal square and initial mixed terms.
The physical part of Q_big, by(28), is

    Q_big,physical=2Lblock
                      -[sum_(i<n_j)(H_j)_i^2+(1+d_j)^2]. (51)

For the mask part, every active block has Kblock/4=2^(j-2) mask queries.
Write P_local_i for the physical prefix inside H_j at a mask query.
The paired contributions of its two opposite blocks are

    Q_big,mask=sum_(i congruent3 mod4,0<=i<Kblock)
                          [2+2P_local_i-Lblock]
                 <=2^(j-2)(A_j-d_j)<2^(j-2)A_j.      (52)

Indeed P_local_i<=S_j=A_j-1, including its last bridge QUERY. This uses
one paired contribution per mask index; both active blocks are already
counted. It is not a per-level renewable correction.
Since Lblock<(3/2)A_j and d_j>(2/5)A_j, (51),(52) imply

    Q_big=Q_big,physical+65536Q_big,mask
       <A_j[3+65536*2^(j-2)-(4/25)A_j].              (53)

By(32), A_j>4000000*2^j, so
(3+65536*2^(j-2))/A_j<1/100. Consequently

    Q_big<-(3/20)A_j^2.

Together with(42),(49),(50), the actual complete certificate obeys

    D_m M_C(q;48)<P_big-(9/80)epsilon*A_j^2
                         <-(9/80)epsilon*A_(m-3)^2<0 (54)

for EVERY m>=1536 and EVERY real q. In particular it fails every
advertised q=zeta/D_m, including1<zeta<250/237. This is an explicit
quantitative whole-word failure. The constant mask pays the old H4 plane,
but its 2^j*A_j compensation cannot pay an A_j^2 physical defect at
large actual j; the precise global normalization and loss are in(50)–(54).
No child PSD, locally replenished slack, conditional covariance payment,
or black-box negative scalar is used.

## 12. Verdict, scope and next interface

Law A has fully declared nonlocal terminal mass and legal remote prefix
flow, but both old N and a separate root right-first coordinate are negative.
Law B uses physical mass, passes the actual root two-query screen, and
still fails old N. Law C changes the actual terminal probabilities at
every query and strictly pays the full old N plus the actual root plane;
the complete original deeper mode(48) has quantitative negative action(54).
Both optimum directions were retained explicitly: the incoming ones are
chosen zero in these three laws, while all outgoing ones remain unchanged
and sum to2. Huber, common, transverse-contact and initial mixed costs
are given exactly in(2)–(8),(18)–(20), not treated as free.

Discard ONLY these THREE independent terminal-reroute distributions with
the exact base flow and rhoRer specified here. The calculation does not
exclude destination-dependent couplings, different prefix transports,
retaining/completing a different base, signed paid reroutes, or the full
terminal-outgoing cone. It also does not prove impossibility of every
fixed-mask distribution. The larger-mode mechanism suggests that a
successful payment must track actual physical mass across scales AND
orient the remote prefix flow to avoid the negative weighted energy(51).
Changing probabilities without altering that bilinear energy is insufficient
for THESE laws. This is a research direction, not a verified necessity
outside their stated class.

No PSD law or unrestricted all-function upper has been constructed.
Accepted5fd396eba75651e7 still supplies ONLY B_(n_m)/D_m<237/250.
No failed array is a primal function, a W lower bound, a W upper bound,
full dual infeasibility, or resolution of the original exact-profile goal.
The original goal remains OPEN.

## 13. Retrieval, resources and persistence

Skills used: query-memory, search-math-results, direct-proving,
construct-counterexamples and identify-key-failures. Plan6256e86b1e0a527a
was published. All subgoals and failures above are in one JOINT UNVERIFIED
derivation; no unverified intermediate is adopted as an accepted brick.
Formal calls and fact_submit are prohibited by the current TASK.md.

Search stage mature_subproblem; active program explicit nonlocal terminal
transport with full coupled prefix flow; missing mechanism original-space
PSD including multiscale loss; mode mutation, intent construction.
One Matlas query returned2309.10340 (projected private-data optimization),
2003.04201 (steps bounded by1/L or line search),2305.02165 (ergodic
primal-dual updates). Their algorithm/hypothesis sets do not apply here.
One web call with two queries retrieved Silver and composability sources.
Exact local downloads/2309.16530.pdf and extracted .txt were read at
Definition2.1, Theorem3.4, Lemmas3.5–3.7 and AppendixA.2 with its proof:
Jason M. Altschuler and Pablo A. Parrilo, Acceleration by Stepsize Hedging II:
Silver Stepsize Schedule for Smooth Convex Optimization, arXiv:2309.16530,
https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.16530 . Its residual is -2Q_ji here. Signed
row accounting and full matrix gluing were methodology only; none of its
Silver/Pell equalities is presumed valid for positive epsilon.
These three laws remain within the already audited original-interpolation
regime; no new external-theorem branch or literature impossibility is used.

Resources: THREE explicit laws; ZERO mathematical programs, numerical
evaluations, arithmetic replays, executed trajectories/recurrences,
optimizers/solvers, benchmark DP, formal calls, fact_submit, new facts,
installations, consults and subagents. Administrative programs do only

sections. Long decorative PDF-text typesetting lines were omitted from the
later display; actual statements and proof lines were read.

Read/write scope: own worker directory and project shared stores.
No other worker private memory, parent configuration, controller counter,
PID/exit state or truth-store file was read or changed. No renewal,
formal gate, external publication, finalization, revocation, shutdown or
original-Goal completion is asserted. The report/handoff belongs to this
ONE allocated invocation. Every mathematical conclusion remains UNVERIFIED.

No worker/service, truth-store, publication/finalization/revocation or Goal operation occurred. Parent owns the ledger and closeout.

## Status

Original exact-profile minimax target OPEN. No target-scale law was obtained even for the entire inherited H_m family. No full-class asymptotic upper certificate was obtained. All new mathematics is UNVERIFIED and is fully derived in PROOF_ATTEMPT.md.
